Beschreibung von dem Lande Kamtschatka (Georg Wilhelm Steller, 1774)
======================================
Steller's 1774 Kamchatka description — the earliest European eyewitness account of Itelmen shamanism, from Bering's expedition naturalist. In German. Public domain. Source: Internet Archive (https://archive.org/details/georgewilhelmste00stel).
